# WF-15 — Study v0 on the lossy sphere

Item briefs and execution history (operator directive 2026-10-01). The §7 row is the status of record.

### §9 item 240 — 2026-10-05 (chain U3)

*Drafted by a general-purpose agent for the 2026-10-05 03:00 daily review; the review reviews it.* Licence: §10 chain U3 (PROJECT_PLAN.md:4622–4625), a weekly-enumerated step (§9 rule (4)); `WF-14` ✅ 2026-10-04 (`20261005T022839Z_WF-14-W1.log`) meets "serial on U2". No band, tolerance, Done-when or validation target moves.

**`WF-15` step 1: `Study` v0 on `MAT-4` step 1's lossy sphere.** One scriptable object composes five things: a geometry generator, a `MAT-1` material, an imposed field, a solve (degree 2 by default) and a SAR result. Assembled this way, it must reproduce `MAT-4` step 1's mean-SAR figure against the direct call, and land inside `MAT-4`'s band, at `-n 2`. Its public names are then registered in `WF-14`'s envelope.
- **Executor:** implementer. New `src/` module plus one additive closed-form helper and the `__all__` export; a new test module; the registry and snapshot. No existing gate module, helper or record is edited.
- **Tier:** W0 / W1 standard, W2 **heavy** (re-declared from the row's "standard by prediction" by pricing below), W3 / W4 the `WF-14` / `WF-13` windows verbatim. `-n 2` throughout, which is the row's letter. Complex build for every solve window.
- **Class:** chain step (solve), measured median ≈ 16 min (2026-10-04 18:00 restock).
- **Cost:** ≈ 16 measured / ≈ 60 predicted min. That is 15 fixed + ≈ 25 code + windows ≈ 1 + 2 + 7 + 0.5 + 1 min + ≈ 8 spare. Tight against the 1-hour timebox, and W2 is most of the compute.
- **Depends on:** nothing open. `WF-14`'s files are on `main`, and nothing named `Study` exists in `src/` (grep, 2026-10-05).

**Settled from the code (read before designing).**
- **`MAT-4` step 1's figure is degree 1, and there is no degree-2 record of it.** The gate solves at `TimeHarmonicSolver(problem, degree=1)` (`tests/validation/test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:189`). The fine rung is h = R/10 in / R/5 out. On the 0.11 image it reads **8.291558e-08 W/kg, 3.536 %** at σ = 0.57 S/m on 74 020 cells (`20260921T111802Z_OPS-55-callers-complex.log.gz:1137`). The same rung reads 8.291558128e-08 to ten digits on the `material_map` path (`20260930T003229Z_MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:494`). The 2026-08-03 figure, 8.291696e-08 on 74 019 cells (`20260803T020448Z_MAT-4-step1-gate.log.gz:499`), is a pre-0.11-image record and cannot be reproduced today. No module solves this fixture at degree 2. `test_lossy_sphere_degree2.py` (`TH-12`) is the `TH-10` Larmor sphere, a different fixture. **Do not invent a degree-2 `MAT-4` record.**
- **How "degree 2 by default" squares with "digit-identical against the direct call".** The comparand is the direct call **at the Study's own degree**, on the same mesh. `MAT-4`'s band is the closed-form gate at either degree. So the item has two legs:
  - the **default leg** (degree 2): the Study equals the direct degree-2 call, and both sit inside `MAT-4`'s band;
  - the **record leg** (`degree=1` passed explicitly): the Study reproduces the degree-1 figure above, which is the only figure `MAT-4` step 1 owns.
- **The direct call** is `MAT-1`'s `_solve_case` `material_map` branch (`tests/materials/test_phantom_material_model.py:360–407`), copied into the new module with a `degree` parameter. The source is hard-coded `degree=1` at `:383` and is not edited. At degree 1 the copy must reproduce `:494`'s map SAR, which proves the copy faithful ((D1) below).
- **The material slot is `MAT-1`'s class, `GelledSalinePhantomMaterial`** (`src/fem_em_solver/materials/phantom.py:22`). It takes either a preset name (`from_preset`, `:43`; presets low / mid / high = (0.45, 80) / (0.75, 78) / (1.05, 76), `:13–18`) or an explicit instance.
  - **`MAT-4`'s σ = 0.57, εᵣ = 78 is not a preset.** The figure legs pass `GelledSalinePhantomMaterial(0.57, 78.0, 64e6)`. `MAT-1`'s (P1) / (P2) showed this assembles bitwise the `material_map` path's arrays and operator (`…MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:485–490`).
  - The preset-by-name route is exercised by a **"mid" leg**, which reproduces `MAT-1`'s own record (`:497`).
  - It goes in as `phantom_material` with **`phantom_tag=1`**. The default 3 raises on this mesh (`core/time_harmonic.py:113`, `:278–282`).
- **The imposed field has no `src/` home.** The quasi-static exterior (uniform + dipole) field exists only as the test-private `_exact_exterior_numpy` (`test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:98–124`). An installed package cannot import `tests/`, which is `WF-13`'s rule.
- **`TimeHarmonicSolver`'s default degree is 1** (`core/time_harmonic.py:318`). The Study must pass its own.

**Code.**
- (1) **`src/fem_em_solver/utils/analytical.py`: new `dielectric_sphere_quasistatic_field(*, radius, epsilon_c, e0=1.0)`.** It returns the `(3, n)` callable.
  - Its arithmetic is **copied verbatim, in the same operation order**, from `test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:104–122`: `beta = (epsilon_c - 1.0) / (epsilon_c + 2.0)`; `e_in = 3.0 * e0 / (epsilon_c + 2.0)`; `common = beta * e0 * R**3 / r_safe**5`; and so on. It returns `dtype=PETSc.ScalarType`.
  - It is additive: no existing function changes.
- (2) **New `src/fem_em_solver/study.py`** with three public names, all frozen dataclasses or a plain class:
  - `SphereInBoxGeometry(sphere_radius, box_half_width, resolution_sphere, resolution_far, sphere_tag=1)`. Its `.build(comm)` wraps `MeshGenerator.sphere_in_box_domain` (`io/mesh.py:2545`).
  - `QuasiStaticSphereField(e0=1.0)`. It builds the Dirichlet callable through (1), from the geometry's radius and ε_c = `complex(epsilon_r, -sigma / (omega * EPSILON_0))`. Here ω = `2.0 * np.pi * frequency_hz`, the same expression as `test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:79` and `phantom.py:122`.
  - `Study(geometry, material, excitation, *, frequency_hz, degree=2, density_kg_m3=1000.0)`. `material` is a `GelledSalinePhantomMaterial` or a preset name. Background is `HomogeneousMaterial(sigma=0.0, epsilon_r=1.0)`, and the boundary is `"pec_zero_tangential_a"` with `dirichlet_e_field`.
    - `.solve(comm=MPI.COMM_WORLD)` builds the mesh and returns a result exposing `mesh`, `cell_tags`, `fields`, `solver` and `degree`.
    - `.mean_sar()` calls `post.sar.mean_sar(fields.e_complex, sigma=fields.sigma_field, rho=density_kg_m3, cell_tags=…, subdomain_ids=sphere_tag, comm=…)` and returns its dict unchanged.
  - **No import from `tests/`**, and no new solver code: the Study only composes existing calls.
- (3) **`src/fem_em_solver/__init__.py`:** export the three names and add them to `__all__` (25 → 28).

From `test_mat5_em_coupled_sphere.py:104`: `L0_BAND`.

**Traps already paid for.**
- **No solve output is ever compared bitwise.**
  - `MAT-1` item 81 went red at 1e-12 on an identical re-run (`known-issues.md:81`).
  - `MAT-5` item 237's (R0c) went red at 2.747e-12 against an unbacked 1e-12 band in one process (`20261005T050759Z_MAT-5-step3-W1-alpha0.log:1874–1882`), today.
  - Bitwise belongs upstream of the solve only: (F), (U), (P).
- **Operation order is part of bitwise.** ω must be `2.0 * np.pi * f`, ε_c must be `complex(εᵣ, -σ / (ω ε₀))`, and the field arithmetic must copy `:104–122`. A rearranged product fails (F) by an ULP, and it is meant to.
- **`phantom_tag` default 3 raises on this mesh.** A tag set in both `material_map` and `phantom_material` raises too (`core/time_harmonic.py:262`). The direct path uses `material_map` only.
- **`TimeHarmonicSolver(problem)` silently means degree 1** (`:318`). (Deg) is there to catch a fall-through.
- **Rank-safety.**
  - DG0 compares use the owned slice `[:size_local]` with reductions (`_dg0_owned_diff`).
  - `_difference` only runs after `_same_pattern` is True on every rank (its collective guard, `:301–303`).
  - `cell_tags.values` is rank-local.
  - Points go through `evaluate_vector_field_parallel`.
- **The `src/` package never imports `tests/`.** `WF-13`'s installed venv has no `/workspace`. W4 catches it.
- **`WF-14` (N): the registry holds node IDs only.** The snapshot is sorted. Adding to `__all__` without both edits makes W3 red by design.
- **`complex_only` on solve tests**, or `WF-14`'s real-build collection errors out (rc 2).
- **Orphan `.c`:** sweep only with `pgrep -c python3` = 0. A killed degree-2 compile poisons its cache entry.
- **Memory:** price by summed `ru_maxrss`, never cgroup `memory.peak` (`TH-12` step 1 caution).
- **No pipe or redirect in the harness command** (the footer records the pipe's status). Status 124 is a finding, not a retry. After a kill, run `pgrep -c python3` first.
- **Rule (i):** no edit to the new module, `study.py`, the registry or the snapshot after its last window.
- **Write ≈, never `~`, in tracked Markdown.**

**Scope.**
- **Covered:** one fixture (R = 0.01 m, h = R/10 / R/5, 64 MHz, σ 0.57 / εᵣ 78 plus the "mid" preset), an imposed quasi-static field, mean SAR over the sphere, `-n 2`.
- **Not claimed:**
  - ports, a coil or YAML (the row);
  - the σ = 0.05 arm and `MAT-4`'s two-σ control at degree 2;
  - h-convergence, or any degree-2 accuracy statement beyond `MAT-4`'s 10 % band;
  - mass-averaged SAR;
  - a stable or semver'd API, sub-package surfaces, or docs (`WF-17`).
- The three names are v0. `WF-16` may extend them.

**Status it can move.** `WF-15` ⬜ → ✅, matching the row's Done-when (rule (j)):
- a `Study` built from a geometry generator (M), a `MAT-1` material (`GelledSalinePhantomMaterial`; preset route (Pre)), an imposed field (F), a solve at degree 2 by default (Deg), and a SAR result (P);
- reproduces `MAT-4` step 1's figure digit-identically against the direct call — (I) at degree 2, and (R) for the figure itself at degree 1;
- inside `MAT-4`'s imported band (G), at `-n 2`;
- entry points registered (W3 green);
- elapsed recorded.

**Two readings in this brief are for the review to accept, or to amend the row in its own commit:**
- "digit-identical" is taken as **bitwise upstream of the solve plus `L0_BAND` 1e-9 on the solve output**, because a bitwise solve output is unattainable at `-n 2` (traps).
- "material preset" is taken as **`MAT-1`'s material class**, because `MAT-4`'s σ = 0.57 is not one of the three presets. The by-name preset is gated separately on `MAT-1`'s record.

If the review rejects either reading, the executor closes on the row: it holds 🟡 and flags the mismatch. The degree-2 SAR printed at `.10e` lands in the row as `WF-15`'s record, which `WF-17` reproduces.

**Negative result.** In every case: report the measurement, park on `attempt/WF-15-*`, mark item 240 BLOCKED in the same commit (rule (d)) and journal it in attempts.md.
- **(F), (U), (P) or (Deg) red:** the Study builds a different problem. This is a code bug; fix and re-run within the slot only if the fix is in the new code, otherwise stop.
- **(D1) red:** the direct copy has drifted from `MAT-1`'s path. Stop.
- **(I) red with (U) and (P) green:** the solve floor exceeds `L0_BAND` here. That is a finding for known-issues.md:81's table. The band is never widened in-slot; the review rules under rule (f).

### §9 item 523 — 2026-10-18 (§10 U60, Study.probe on a thermal-only Study; chain step, solve)

*Drafted by a read-only agent for the 2026-10-18 03:00 daily review; the review reviews it. Code read on `d94320db`; nothing was run.*

**Licence.** §10 chain U, U60 (`PROJECT_PLAN.md:4855–4860`): "`Study.probe` on a thermal-only Study (the `WF-13` design gap): point probes on the ball and box Studies reproduce `MAT-19`'s virtual-probe record and the Robin series at the points within `INTERIOR_BAND`; the three helpers that raise raw exceptions raise `StudyConfigError` naming the field, with controls. Serial on U59 for the box half." The gap was read from source by the 2026-10-17 10:30 review (`docs/planning/chunks/WF-13.md:699–707`). Chain U's target applies. **No band, tolerance, record, Done-when or target is set or moved.** Owner: `WF-15`, which owns `Study.probe` (step 4, item 326, this file `:358–404`); this is `WF-15` **step 6** (step 5 is item 521a, `docs/planning/chunks/WF-1.md` "§9 item 521"). Templates: items 326, 330 and 507.

**Findings at drafting (source only, not run).**
1. **`Study.probe` cannot reach a thermal-only temperature.** `_study_probe` calls `self.temperature(cooling)` with no transient (`src/fem_em_solver/study.py:1539`). On a `ThermalBallGeometry` that dispatches to `_study_transient_temperature` (`:1253–1254`), which raises `TypeError` when `transient` is `None` (`:1446–1447`), before any mesh. The fix is a keyword pass-through, not a new algorithm: `ball_average` (`thermal/probe.py:168–171`) works on any P1 θ.
2. **`MAT-19`'s record cannot be reproduced to its digits on a ball Study.** `MAT-19`'s (T) 1.7315e-03 is a maximum over 3 probes × 40 times on `MAT-12`'s EM-solved sphere-in-box tag-1 submesh, 19 496 cells (`20261005T095808Z_MAT-19-step1-W1.log.gz:203`; `tests/validation/test_mat19_virtual_temperature_probe.py:83`). A `ThermalBallGeometry` is a gmsh `ball_domain` (`study.py:1343–1348`), a different mesh, and `Study.probe` returns one reading per probe at the end time, not a series (`ProbeStudyResult`, `:1509–1519`).
   - What *can* be reproduced is `MAT-19`'s **gate**: its exact Helmholtz ball-mean comparand `_ball_mean` (`test_mat19_…py:117–127`) and its band, at t\* on the same physics (R, k, h, ρc, q_cf, probes).
   - Item 326 scoped exactly this out: "Not claimed: … `MAT-19`'s (T) 1 % gate (it is on the q_cf transient, not reproduced here)" (this file, `:392`). This item closes that.
   - A time series through the Study (snapshots) is not posed.
3. **The band name.** `MAT-19`'s band is `P_BAND` = 1e-2 (`tests/validation/test_mat10_sar_to_heat_sphere.py:59`, imported at `test_mat19_…py:56`). U60 says `INTERIOR_BAND`, which is also 1e-2 but is `MAT-13`'s constant, the one `MAT-21` uses. The drafter imports each half's **own** constant: `P_BAND` for the ball (`MAT-19`'s gate) and `INTERIOR_BAND` for the box (`MAT-21`'s). The values are equal and no band moves (question 2).
4. **"Point probes"** is read as probes *centred at* points. `Study.probe` is `MAT-19`'s ball average (an owned-cell integral). Scalar point evaluation on the box is P37's (`MAT-21` step 10, `PROJECT_PLAN.md:4808–4816`) and is not claimed.
5. **The box comparand at a probe is exact.** On the steady box with uniform q, ∇²θ = −Q/K, so θ + Q\|x − c\|²/(6K) is harmonic. The ball mean over B(c, a) is therefore θ̄ = θ_series(c) − Q a²/(10K) whenever the ball lies inside the box (mean of \|x − c\|² over the ball = 3a²/5). This is the identity `MAT-19`'s `_ball_mean` steady term carries for the sphere (`steady … − 3a²/5`, `test_mat19_…py:126`). The truncated series obeys it to its truncation deficit, 4.6708e-6 (`COMPARAND_FLOOR`, `tests/validation/test_mat21_box_fem_p2.py:83`).
6. **The three helpers** (`study.py:155–168`) on a thermal-only Study, predicted:
   - `omega` → `TypeError` (`2.0 * np.pi * None`);
   - `epsilon_c()` → `AttributeError` (`None.epsilon_r`);
   - `dirichlet_field()` → `AttributeError` (`'UniformHeatSource' object has no attribute 'dirichlet_field'`).
   W0 observes them before any edit (item 507's pattern).
7. **`StudyConfigError` lives in `study_yaml.py`** (`src/fem_em_solver/study_yaml.py:158–165`, a `ValueError` subclass with `(source, field, reason)`), and `study_yaml` imports `study` at module level. So `study.py` can raise it only through a **function-local** import, which is cycle-safe at call time. Every sibling thermal-only refusal raises `TypeError` (`:171–172`, `:533–537`, `:1446–1447`), so the letter's `StudyConfigError` makes these three a `ValueError` among `TypeError`s (question 1).

**Traps already paid for.**
- **`MAT-19`'s:** the probe is an owned-cell integral, with no point owner. Every rank enters the one allreduce (item 227's deadlock). Ghost values are needed; ghost cells are never summed. Centres are plain floats (`POST-8` 3a). Use θ, not T (item 258).
- **The thermal degree is 1** (`RobinCooling.degree`), never `Study.degree`.
- **The real build here; the re-gates in complex.** Never `float.hex` across builds; (H)'s hex checks are within one process.
- **The function-local import** of `StudyConfigError` must stay inside the helper. A module-level `from .study_yaml import …` in `study.py` is an import cycle (`study_yaml.py` imports `study` at load).
- **The control patches `fem_em_solver.study._refuse_thermal_only_attr`**, so the three call sites must look the name up globally (item 507's trap).
- **The `-c` quoting** (W0): no single quote inside `bash -lc '…'`.

### Review ruling 2026-11-05 03:00 — U127 runs in the complex build, through the `Study`'s single-port fields

The 11-04 weekly enumerated U127. Five corrections are written here as execution detail; the target is kept. **Two of them are new public surface: the `drive=` keyword, and `thermal:` on the four-port rod.** They are named in the dashboard for the 11-07 weekly to ratify or revert.

1. **The build is complex, not real.** The EM half is a four-port time-harmonic solve.
2. **The drive is one port, `P1` at 1 V.** The four-port `Study` has no ccw superposition (item 664 §2; `Study.sar` is per single-port drive, `study.py:2543–2606`). One port at 1 V is the page's convention.
3. **Arm M stays on the test side.** A kept rod with tag 403 as gel through the `Study` is the public decision the 11-03 10:30 ruling (1) left open. So arm R goes through the `Study`, and arm M is built test-side on the same mesh family.
4. **The ratio is printed as R / M.** I41's 1.10 is R / M (`20261025T201613Z_IMP-3-I41-W1b.log:3776–3777`, 1.104515 / 1.108684, under ccw, on the ragged mesh), not M / R.
5. **MAT-15's slope (S2) is a whole-volume identity.** On a tip 1 g ball it also picks up a conduction term. In review arithmetic that term is ≈ 1e-5 to 1e-3, which straddles `S2_BAND`. It is asserted at the band, and the slope at Δt₁/10 is printed beside it.

### Review note 2026-11-05 18:00 — item 831 BLOCKED, left to the 11-07 weekly (not re-posed)

- **The red is the brief's own named negative**, so its unblock is the weekly's ("the weekly re-poses the band"). This review does not re-pose it under rule (6): both candidate re-poses move what (S2-tip) compares against — a wider band, or a mass-consistent comparand — and rule (6) never moves a band or a target.
- **The brief's premise is contradicted by the measurement.** It read (S2-tip) red as "the conduction term". At Δt₁/10 the top-tip deficit moves only 4.3368e-03 → 4.2166e-03 and the bottom 5.9425e-04 → 6.8113e-04, while (S2) scales 4.8353e-06 → 4.8365e-07 (`20261105T212316Z_WF-15-s14-W1-r3.log`). A conduction term would scale with Δt. The executor's reading: the first step's 1 g ball mean of a P1 θ under the consistent mass is a mass-matrix-weighted mean of the DG0 q, not the ball's own q̄ — a Δt-independent difference largest where q varies most (the top tip). **For the weekly:** (a) re-pose (S2-tip)'s band, or (b) assert the tip slope against the mass-consistent mean (Δt·mean(M⁻¹b)/ρc on the ball) at `S2_BAND` with q̄ printed beside — (b) keeps the band and changes the comparand.
- **Disclosure carried:** W1 crashed twice in non-gate code before any gate value (a `src` `running_average` on a one-step series; a test-side facet-tag lookup) and was fixed and re-run twice; the 10-31 weekly's narrow ruling allows one. Nothing closes on those logs (the item is parked); the weekly may rule whether the second re-run stands.
- Branch `attempt/WF-15-s14-20261105T212843Z` (`b58a9b20`) is kept for the re-pose. Item 839 (U128) stays blocked on it.
